######## 9. THE PLAN SCRIPT (stage 3's run_plan.py with only the seeds changed)

==== THE PLAN SCRIPT (stage 3's run_plan.py with only the seeds changed): run_plan.py (sha256 a282f98ea613745678e08d20a2fe277de1dcb6a962ac83fd7196422b195c3c93, 5839 bytes) ====
"""(R1 + R2 follow-up, INDEPENDENT RE-RUN #59, a copy of stage 3 run_plan.py with only the seeds changed) ONE plan of EVERY arm on seeds
5561-5564, run from the WORKTREE itself (not a snapshot: the ledger's begin hashed this tree), each job through runcmd.py
(the command, its start and end time and its exit code saved under OUT/logs/), with its dependencies, at most N at once.

    py -3.12 run_plan.py OUT [N]

Per seed (16 jobs; 64 in all):
  CONTROL, K-AFTER, K-INSIDE, K-SILENT, K-FIRST                          (no dependency)
  K-BETWEEN (rule new) and K-BETWEEN-MID (the diagnostic midpoint)        after CONTROL
  K-INSIDE-OLD, K-SILENT-OLD (mutant R1-M)                                after their twin
  LAUNCH, LAUNCH-OLD (mutant S27)                                         after K-AFTER
  CHAIN, CHAIN-OLD (mutant CH1), CHAIN-R1-OLD (mutant FX1)                after K-SILENT (orchestrator ruling 1)
  R2-RETRY                                                                after K-AFTER
  R2-RETRY-OLD (mutant FX3)                                               after R2-RETRY
Bounded: every job is a child with its own timeouts (child A 1200 s, child B 300 s, CHAIN's B 300 s, the launcher's); the
whole plan is abandoned past 3 hours (its children killed by PID: only processes this plan started).
"""
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
W = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_810d6b51-d6f-1")
BR = "C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/sandbox-pokebot"
FIRST, LAST = 5561, 5564
LIVE = "ai_player/gates/live_r1.py"


def plan_heldout(out: Path) -> list[dict]:
    rows = out / "rows"
    jobs = []
    for seed in range(FIRST, LAST + 1):
        jobs.append({"id": f"CONTROL-{seed}", "args": ["control", "--seed", str(seed)], "after": []})
        for arm in ("K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST"):
            jobs.append({"id": f"{arm}-{seed}", "args": ["arm", "--arm", arm, "--seed", str(seed)], "after": []})
    for seed in range(FIRST, LAST + 1):
        control = str(rows / f"CONTROL-{seed}.json")
        k_after = str(rows / f"K-AFTER-{seed}.json")
        k_silent = str(rows / f"K-SILENT-{seed}.json")
        jobs.append({"id": f"K-BETWEEN-{seed}", "after": [f"CONTROL-{seed}"],
                     "args": ["arm", "--arm", "K-BETWEEN", "--seed", str(seed), "--between-from", control,
                              "--between-rule", "new"]})
        jobs.append({"id": f"K-BETWEEN-MID-{seed}", "after": [f"CONTROL-{seed}"],
                     "args": ["arm", "--arm", "K-BETWEEN", "--tag", "K-BETWEEN-MID", "--seed", str(seed),
                              "--between-from", control, "--between-rule", "midpoint"]})
        for twin in ("K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT"):
            jobs.append({"id": f"{twin}-OLD-{seed}", "after": [f"{twin}-{seed}"],
                         "args": ["old", "--twin-row", str(rows / f"{twin}-{seed}.json")]})
        for old in (False, True):
            name = "LAUNCH-OLD" if old else "LAUNCH"
            jobs.append({"id": f"{name}-{seed}", "after": [f"K-AFTER-{seed}"],
                         "args": ["launch", "--from-row", k_after] + (["--old"] if old else [])})
        jobs.append({"id": f"CHAIN-{seed}", "after": [f"K-SILENT-{seed}"], "args": ["chain", "--from-row", k_silent]})
        for old in ("CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD"):
            jobs.append({"id": f"{old}-{seed}", "after": [f"K-SILENT-{seed}"],
                         "args": ["chain", "--old", old, "--from-row", k_silent]})
        jobs.append({"id": f"R2-RETRY-{seed}", "after": [f"K-AFTER-{seed}"], "args": ["r2", "--from-row", k_after]})
        jobs.append({"id": f"R2-RETRY-OLD-{seed}", "after": [f"R2-RETRY-{seed}"],
                     "args": ["r2", "--old", "--from-row", str(rows / f"R2-RETRY-{seed}.json")]})
    return jobs


def main() -> int:
    out = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
    limit = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 12
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    jobs = plan_heldout(out)
    pending = {job["id"]: job for job in jobs}
    running: dict[str, subprocess.Popen] = {}
    done: dict[str, int] = {}
    began = time.monotonic()
    print(f"PLAN heldout: {len(jobs)} jobs, at most {limit} at once, tree {W}, out {out} "
          f"({time.strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')})", flush=True)
    while pending or running:
        if time.monotonic() - began > 3 * 3600:
            print("PLAN ABANDONED: over 3 hours", flush=True)
            for proc in running.values():
                proc.kill()
            return 9
        for job_id, proc in list(running.items()):
            code = proc.poll()
            if code is not None:
                done[job_id] = code
                del running[job_id]
                print(f"  done {job_id}: exit {code} ({time.strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')})", flush=True)
        ready = [j for j in pending.values() if all(d in done for d in j["after"])]
        for job in ready[:max(0, limit - len(running))]:
            del pending[job["id"]]
            cmd = [sys.executable, str(S / "runcmd.py"), str(out / "logs" / f"{job['id']}.out"), "--cwd", str(W),
                   "--env", f"AI_PLAYER_BUILD_ROOT={BR}", "--env", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1", "--",
                   "py", "-3.12", "-B", LIVE, *job["args"], "--out", str(out)]
            running[job["id"]] = subprocess.Popen(cmd)
            print(f"  start {job['id']} ({time.strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')})", flush=True)
        time.sleep(2)
    bad = {k: v for k, v in done.items() if v != 0}
    print(f"PLAN DONE: {len(done)} jobs, exit codes not 0: {bad or 'none'} ({time.strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')}, "
          f"{time.monotonic() - began:.0f} s)", flush=True)
    return 1 if bad else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

######## 13. MY INSTRUMENT: mine.py

==== MY INSTRUMENT: mine.py: mine.py (sha256 63a30aab3117588ee791a198741a4f602850234796092acacd9f2ce3c9f745da, 10860 bytes) ====
"""The INDEPENDENT RE-RUN's own instrument parts (imports nothing from ai_player): a flash parser with sector checksums,
a strict memory.jsonl reader that rebuilds save payloads from their chains, an event-driven replay of rule N
(orchestrator rulings 11-12), and small helpers. Written by the re-runner; the parser is adapted from the R1 + R2
verification's my_discriminators.py (itself fault-tested), the reader from its r1_live.py, made STRICT (a delete must
find its key; a set must go through objects only; an unknown op or key raises).
"""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import struct
from pathlib import Path

SIG = 0x08012025
SECTOR = 0x1000
SIZES = {0: 0xF2C, 1: 0xF80, 2: 0xF80, 3: 0xF80, 4: 0x3D88 - 3 * 0xF80}
for _i in range(5, 14):
    SIZES[_i] = min(0xF80, 0x83D0 - (_i - 5) * 0xF80)


class ReaderError(Exception):
    pass


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ flash
def _checksum(data: bytes, size: int) -> int:
    total = sum(struct.unpack_from(f"<{size // 4}I", data, 0)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return ((total >> 16) + total) & 0xFFFF


def parse_sav_bytes(raw: bytes) -> dict:
    if len(raw) < 32 * SECTOR:
        return {"error": f"size {len(raw)}"}
    slots = {}
    for slot in (0, 1):
        found = []
        for phys in range(slot * 14, slot * 14 + 14):
            sec = raw[phys * SECTOR:(phys + 1) * SECTOR]
            sid, chk, sig, counter = struct.unpack_from("<HHII", sec, 0xFF4)
            if sig != SIG:
                continue
            ok = sid in SIZES and _checksum(sec, SIZES[sid]) == chk
            found.append({"phys": phys, "id": sid, "counter": counter, "chk_ok": ok, "data": sec})
        slots[slot] = found
    out = {"slots": {}, "complete": [], "partial": []}
    for slot, found in slots.items():
        good = [f for f in found if f["chk_ok"]]
        counters = sorted({f["counter"] for f in good})
        ids = sorted({f["id"] for f in good})
        full = len(good) == 14 and ids == list(range(14)) and len(counters) == 1
        out["slots"][slot] = {"signatures": len(found), "checksum_ok": len(good), "counters": counters, "complete": full}
        if full:
            out["complete"].append((counters[0], slot))
        elif found:
            out["partial"].append((max(counters) if counters else None, slot))
    if out["complete"]:
        counter, slot = max(out["complete"])
        sec0 = next(f for f in slots[slot] if f["id"] == 0)["data"]
        sec1 = next(f for f in slots[slot] if f["id"] == 1)["data"]
        hours, minutes, seconds, frames = struct.unpack_from("<HBBB", sec0, 0x0E)
        x, y = struct.unpack_from("<hh", sec1, 0)
        group, num = struct.unpack_from("<bb", sec1, 4)
        count = sec1[0x234]
        pids = [f"{struct.unpack_from('<I', sec1, 0x238 + 100 * i)[0]:08x}" for i in range(min(count, 6))]
        out["loaded"] = {"counter": counter, "slot": slot, "play_time": [hours, minutes, seconds, frames],
                         "map": [group, num], "pos": [x, y], "pids": pids}
    return out


def parse_sav(path: Path) -> dict:
    return parse_sav_bytes(path.read_bytes()) if path and Path(path).is_file() else {"error": "no file"}


def file_word(p: dict) -> str:
    """EMPTY (no signature); CORRUPT (signatures, no complete slot); S<counter> of the newest complete slot, +ERROR when
    the other slot holds a partial write with a HIGHER counter."""
    if "error" in p:
        return "UNREADABLE"
    if not any(s["signatures"] for s in p["slots"].values()):
        return "EMPTY"
    if not p["complete"]:
        return "CORRUPT"
    c = max(p["complete"])[0]
    bad = any(pc is not None and pc > c for pc, _ in p["partial"])
    return f"S{c}" + ("+ERROR" if bad else "")


def loaded_is(p: dict, truth: dict | None) -> bool:
    """The loaded slot holds `truth` exactly: play time (all four fields), map, tile and party PIDs."""
    if not truth or "loaded" not in p:
        return False
    return all(p["loaded"][k] == truth.get(k) for k in ("play_time", "map", "pos", "pids"))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ memory.jsonl
def read_lines(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for n, raw in enumerate(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
        if raw.strip():
            try:
                out.append(json.loads(raw))
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise ReaderError(f"line {n} is not JSON: {exc}") from exc
    return out


def _put(tree: dict, path: list, value) -> None:
    node = tree
    for key in path[:-1]:
        if key not in node:
            node[key] = {}
        if not isinstance(node[key], dict):
            raise ReaderError(f"set path {path!r:.100} goes through a non-object at {key!r}")
        node = node[key]
    node[path[-1]] = copy.deepcopy(value)


def _drop(tree: dict, path: list) -> None:
    node = tree
    for key in path[:-1]:
        node = node.get(key) if isinstance(node, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            raise ReaderError(f"del path {path!r:.100} does not exist (at {key!r})")
    if not isinstance(node, dict) or path[-1] not in node:
        raise ReaderError(f"del path {path!r:.100}: key {path[-1]!r} not found")
    del node[path[-1]]


def apply_diff(parent: dict, diff: dict) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(diff, dict):
        raise ReaderError(f"a diff that is not an object: {type(diff).__name__}")
    unknown = set(diff) - {"set", "del"}
    if unknown:
        raise ReaderError(f"unknown diff keys {sorted(unknown)}")
    child = copy.deepcopy(parent)
    for op in diff.get("del") or []:
        path = op[0] if (isinstance(op, list) and op and isinstance(op[0], list)) else op
        if not isinstance(path, list) or not path:
            raise ReaderError(f"unknown del op {op!r:.100}")
        _drop(child, path)
    for op in diff.get("set") or []:
        if not (isinstance(op, list) and len(op) == 2 and isinstance(op[0], list) and op[0]):
            raise ReaderError(f"unknown set op {op!r:.100}")
        _put(child, op[0], op[1])
    return child


def rebuild(lines: list[dict], save_id: int) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, list[int]]:
    """Rebuild save `save_id`'s payload by walking its parent chain back to a full save, then applying each diff forward.
    Returns (payload, error, chain ids newest first)."""
    saves = {}
    for line in lines:
        if line.get("kind") == "save":
            if line.get("id") in saves:
                return None, f"duplicate save id {line.get('id')}", []
            saves[line.get("id")] = line
    chain, cur, seen = [], save_id, set()
    while True:
        if cur not in saves:
            return None, f"save id {cur} not in the file (chain {chain})", chain
        if cur in seen:
            return None, f"a cycle at {cur}", chain
        seen.add(cur)
        chain.append(cur)
        line = saves[cur]
        if line.get("full"):
            break
        cur = line.get("parent")
        if cur is None:
            return None, f"diff save {chain[-1]} has no parent", chain
    payload = None
    try:
        for sid in reversed(chain):
            line = saves[sid]
            payload = copy.deepcopy(line["memory"]) if line.get("full") else apply_diff(payload, line["memory"])
    except (ReaderError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
        return None, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", chain
    return payload, None, chain


def last_session(lines: list[dict] | None) -> dict | None:
    return next((x for x in reversed(lines or []) if x.get("kind") == "session"), None)


def save_lines(lines: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    return [x for x in lines if x.get("kind") == "save"]


def line_by_stamp(lines: list[dict], truth: dict | None, min_id: int = 0) -> list[int]:
    """Save line ids (>= min_id) whose [hours, minutes] stamp equals the truth's RAM play time's first two fields."""
    if not truth:
        return []
    want = list(truth["play_time"][:2])
    return [x["id"] for x in save_lines(lines) if x["id"] >= min_id and list(x.get("play_time") or [])[:2] == want]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ rule N (orchestrator rulings 11-12), event-driven
def replay_n(saves: list[dict], maps: list, starts: list[int], arrived: int | None, known_a: set, offset: int = 30) -> dict:
    """Walk B's record forward in time. A save becomes the anchor when it COMPLETES (its e4); from then a START press
    blocks that anchor until the next completed save re-anchors; a map entry after the anchor's e4 onto a map in neither
    `known_a` nor B's maps (from the held arrival) up to the anchor's e3 MEETS the rule while the anchor is not blocked.
    At equal frames a START is taken before a map entry and a completion after both (it re-anchors from its own e4 on)."""
    if arrived is None:
        return {"met": False, "why": "no held arrival", "log": []}
    events = []
    for s in saves:
        if s.get("e4") is not None and s.get("e3") is not None:
            events.append((s["e4"], 2, "save", s))
    for f in starts:
        events.append((f, 0, "start", f))
    for f, key in maps:
        if f >= arrived:
            events.append((f, 1, "map", key))
    events.sort(key=lambda e: (e[0], e[1]))
    anchor, blocked, log = None, False, []
    from_arrival = [(f, k) for f, k in maps if f >= arrived]
    for frame, _order, kind, what in events:
        if kind == "save":
            if anchor is not None and not blocked:
                log.append({"anchor": anchor["save"], "superseded_at": frame})
            anchor, blocked = what, False
        elif kind == "start" and anchor is not None and frame > anchor["e4"] and not blocked:
            blocked = True
            log.append({"anchor": anchor["save"], "blocked_by_start": frame})
        elif kind == "map" and anchor is not None and not blocked and frame > anchor["e4"]:
            known = set(known_a) | {k for f, k in from_arrival if f <= anchor["e3"]}
            if what not in known:
                return {"met": True, "anchor": anchor["save"], "e3": anchor["e3"], "e4": anchor["e4"], "entered": frame,
                        "new_map": what, "kill_at": frame + offset, "known": sorted(known), "log": log}
    return {"met": False, "why": "no anchor met before the record ended", "log": log}


def first_frames(maps: list, lo: int | None = None) -> dict:
    first: dict = {}
    for f, key in maps:
        if lo is None or f >= lo:
            first.setdefault(key, f)
    return first


def sha16(path: Path) -> str | None:
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()[:16] if path and Path(path).is_file() else None


def sha64(path: Path) -> str | None:
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest() if path and Path(path).is_file() else None

######## 14. MY INSTRUMENT: my_facts.py

==== MY INSTRUMENT: my_facts.py: my_facts.py (sha256 8f00eda253e241e3bcc451cacb116c4f12053b79a259bda1f7341866e6948a12, 26837 bytes) ====
"""INDEPENDENT facts for every arm, from the RAW outputs only (never live_r1's grade, notes, summary or verdict): frozen
.sav bytes (mine.parse_sav), frozen memory.jsonl files (mine.rebuild), the per-frame records in each row (child A's
a.*, child B's b.save_watch / b.kill / b.presses / b.arrival / b.boot), the runs' actions.jsonl / world.jsonl /
purity.json, launches.jsonl, and the mutant trees' files on disk.

    py -3.12 -I my_facts.py OUT FIRST LAST    -> prints the facts and 9 discriminators per seed; writes my-facts-FIRST-LAST.json

Each arm's facts are a function of its row so the fault test (fault_mine.py) can call them on planted copies.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import mine  # noqa: E402

W = Path("C:/Remote Projects/Pokebot_Gen3_Enhancement/.claude/worktrees/wf_810d6b51-d6f-1/ai_player")
REAL = {"game_memory.py": mine.sha16(W / "game_memory.py"), "mode.py": mine.sha16(W / "mode.py"),
        "host/launcher.py": mine.sha16(W / "host/launcher.py"), "skills/saving.py": mine.sha16(W / "skills/saving.py")}
N_OFFSET = 30          # N_KILL_FRAMES (spec 5.5: fixed)
OLD_KILL_AFTER_E4 = 40  # CHAIN_OLD_KILL 'S1.e4+40' (orchestrator ruling 3: B's own first save's e4 + 40)
STOP_ARRIVAL = 57       # R2_STOP_ARRIVAL as registered (stage 2's measurement)


def row(out: Path, name: str, seed: int):
    p = out / "rows" / f"{name}-{seed}.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.is_file() else None


def new_maps_a(a: dict) -> list:
    """Child A's NEW: maps first stood on from S1's e3 up to S2's e3 (or the kill when there is no S2)."""
    saves, kill = a.get("saves") or [], (a.get("kill") or {}).get("frame")
    if not saves:
        return []
    lo = saves[0]["e3"]
    hi = saves[1]["e3"] if len(saves) > 1 else kill
    if hi is None:
        return []
    return sorted(k for k, f in mine.first_frames(a.get("maps") or []).items() if lo <= f < hi)


def kill_facts(r: dict) -> dict:
    a = r.get("a") or {}
    kill, saves = a.get("kill") or {}, a.get("saves") or []
    kf, spec = kill.get("frame"), kill.get("spec") or ""
    which = 1 if spec.startswith("S2") else 0
    sec = (saves[which].get("sectors") or {}) if len(saves) > which else {}
    line = (saves[which].get("line") or {}) if len(saves) > which else {}
    return {"kill_frame": kf, "spec": spec, "new_sectors_at_kill": sum(1 for f in sec.values() if kf is not None and f <= kf),
            "line_frame": line.get("frame"), "line_seen_at": line.get("seen_at"),
            "line_before_kill": line.get("seen_at") is not None and kf is not None and line.get("seen_at") < kf}


def menu_facts(b: dict) -> dict:
    presses = b.get("presses") or []
    on_menu = [p for p in presses if isinstance(p, list) and len(p) > 2 and p[2] and p[2][0] == "Task_HandleMainMenuInput"]
    cont = [p for p in on_menu if p[1] == "A" and p[2][2] == 0 and (p[2][1] or 0) >= 1]
    newg = [p for p in on_menu if p[1] == "A" and (p[2][2] != 0 or (p[2][1] or 0) < 1)]
    return {"continue_pressed": bool(cont), "no_new_game": not newg and not (b.get("new_game_tasks") or []),
            "no_page": not (b.get("pages") or []), "pages": [pg.get("symbol") for pg in b.get("pages") or []]}


def ident(b: dict, truth: dict | None) -> dict:
    arr, boot = b.get("arrival") or {}, b.get("boot") or {}
    if not truth:
        return {"ok": False, "why": "no truth"}
    got = {"pids": arr.get("pids") == truth.get("pids"), "map": arr.get("map") == truth.get("map"),
           "pos": arr.get("pos") == truth.get("pos"), "boot_play_time": boot.get("play_time") == truth.get("play_time")}
    return {"ok": all(got.values()), **got}


def restore_from_run(run_dir) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    """The world snapshot written at the restore: the index of the action log's 'world snapshot' record whose reason
    starts 'restored from save', in world.jsonl."""
    if not run_dir:
        return None, None
    run = Path(run_dir)
    acts, world = run / "actions.jsonl", run / "world.jsonl"
    if not acts.is_file() or not world.is_file():
        return None, None
    idx = -1
    for raw in acts.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        rec = json.loads(raw)
        if rec.get("decision") == "world" and rec.get("action") == "snapshot":
            idx += 1
            if str(rec.get("reason", "")).startswith("restored from save"):
                lines = world.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
                return (json.loads(lines[idx]) if idx < len(lines) else None), rec["reason"]
    return None, None


def mutant_facts(r: dict, b: dict, rel: str, sha_key: str) -> dict:
    """The child ran the mutant copy: the file it reports is under the row's mutant root, its sha256 (MY hash of the file
    on disk) equals what it reports and differs from the real tree's, and the file holds every edit's replacement."""
    m = r.get("mutant") or {}
    root = Path(m.get("root") or "/nonexistent")
    rep = (b.get(sha_key) or {})
    path = Path(rep.get("file") or "/nonexistent")
    mine_sha = mine.sha16(path)
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.is_file() else ""
    edits = [e for e in m.get("edits") or [] if e[0] == rel]
    return {"under_root": str(path).lower().startswith(str(root).lower()), "sha_mine": mine_sha,
            "sha_reported": rep.get("sha256"), "real": REAL.get(rel),
            "ok": bool(edits) and str(path).lower().startswith(str(root).lower()) and mine_sha == rep.get("sha256")
            and mine_sha != REAL.get(rel) and all(e[2] in text for e in edits)}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the existing arms (as the R1 verification measured them)
def k_arm_facts(r: dict) -> dict:
    a = r.get("a") or {}
    sav = Path((r.get("frozen") or {}).get("dir") or "/x") / "current_save.sav"
    p = mine.parse_sav(sav)
    f = {"file": mine.file_word(p), "loaded": p.get("loaded"), "kill": kill_facts(r), "NEW": new_maps_a(a)}
    sess = mine.last_session(r.get("b_memory"))
    f["restored_maps"] = sorted((sess or {}).get("restored_maps") or []) if sess else None
    f["continued_from"] = (sess or {}).get("continued_from")
    f["NEW_in_restored"] = sorted(m for m in f["NEW"] if m in (f["restored_maps"] or []))
    truths = a.get("truths") or {}
    b = r.get("b") or {}
    f["arrival_eq"] = {s: ident(b, truths[s])["ok"] for s in truths}
    f.update(menu_facts(b))
    f["b_reason"] = str((b.get("play") or {}).get("reason"))[:100]
    f["skills_saving"] = (b.get("skills_saving") or {}).get("file", "")[-70:]
    f["skills_saving_sha"] = (b.get("skills_saving") or {}).get("sha256")
    f["skills_saving_sha_mine"] = mine.sha16(Path((b.get("skills_saving") or {}).get("file") or "/x"))
    return f


def control_facts(r: dict) -> dict:
    a = r.get("a") or {}
    p = mine.parse_sav(Path(r["profile"]) / "current_save.sav")
    n = len(a.get("saves") or [])
    t = (a.get("truths") or {}).get(f"S{n}")
    return {"file": mine.file_word(p), "saves": n, "loaded_is_newest_truth": mine.loaded_is(p, t),
            "end": (a.get("play") or {}).get("reason")}


def launch_facts(r: dict) -> dict:
    run = Path(r["launch"]["run_dir"])
    acts = [json.loads(x) for x in (run / "actions.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    st = next((x for x in acts if x.get("decision") == "start"), None)
    sess = mine.last_session(r.get("memory_after"))
    base = (sess or {}).get("frame_base")
    pur = run / "purity.json"
    return {"first_look": [((st or {}).get("observation") or {}).get("screen", {}).get("game_state"),
                           (st["frame"] - base) if st and base is not None else None],
            "continued_from": (sess or {}).get("continued_from"),
            "purity": json.loads(pur.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("verdict") if pur.is_file() else None,
            "mutant": bool(r.get("mutant"))}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ CHAIN, CHAIN-OLD, CHAIN-R1-OLD
def rule_n_part(b: dict, sw: dict, known_a: list) -> dict:
    """Rule N re-derived by ME from B's record: its saves (e3, e4), its map changes (from the held arrival: the map it
    stood on at the arrival, then every later change), its START presses; compared with the watcher's kill."""
    f = {}
    bsaves = sw.get("saves") or []
    kill = b.get("kill") or {}
    starts = sorted(s["f"] for s in sw.get("starts") or [] if s.get("f") is not None)
    arrived = sw.get("arrived")
    maps_all = sw.get("maps") or []
    if arrived is not None:
        before = [m for m in maps_all if m[0] <= arrived]
        from_arrival = ([[arrived, before[-1][1]]] if before else []) + [list(m) for m in maps_all if m[0] > arrived]
    else:
        from_arrival = []
    f["_from_arrival"] = from_arrival
    f["maps_from_arrival_matches_watcher"] = from_arrival == [list(m) for m in sw.get("maps_from_arrival") or []]
    rep = mine.replay_n(bsaves, from_arrival, starts, arrived, set(known_a), N_OFFSET)
    f["replay"] = {k: rep.get(k) for k in ("met", "anchor", "e3", "e4", "entered", "new_map", "kill_at", "why", "log")}
    f["watcher_agrees"] = bool(rep.get("met")) and kill.get("anchor") == rep.get("anchor") and kill.get("new_map") == rep.get("new_map") \
        and kill.get("entered") == rep.get("entered") and kill.get("frame") == rep.get("kill_at")
    k = rep.get("anchor")
    sk = next((s for s in bsaves if s["save"] == k), None) if k is not None else None
    f["sk"] = {"save": k, "e3": (sk or {}).get("e3"), "e4": (sk or {}).get("e4"), "truth": (sk or {}).get("truth")}
    f["sk_e4_before_kill"] = sk is not None and kill.get("frame") is not None and sk["e4"] < kill["frame"]
    f["starts_since_sk_e4"] = [s for s in starts if sk is not None and kill.get("frame") is not None and sk["e4"] < s <= kill["frame"]]
    f["b_saves"] = [{"save": s["save"], "e3": s.get("e3"), "e4": s.get("e4"), "at": (s.get("start") or {}).get("const")} for s in bsaves]
    f["b_frames"] = b.get("frames")
    f["arrived"] = arrived
    return f


def chain_facts(r: dict) -> dict:
    name = r.get("name") or r["arm"]
    ks = r.get("ks") or {}
    a = ks.get("a") or {}
    asaves = a.get("saves") or []
    s1_e3 = asaves[0]["e3"] if asaves else None
    known_a = sorted({k for fr, k in a.get("maps") or [] if s1_e3 is not None and fr <= s1_e3})
    truths_a = a.get("truths") or {}
    f = {"arm": name, "known_a": known_a, "known_a_equals_parents": known_a == sorted((r.get("chain") or {}).get("known_maps") or [])}
    # the K-SILENT frozen memory, and the ids of S1 and S2 by identity (their stamps = child A's RAM truths)
    ks_mem_path = Path((ks.get("frozen") or {}).get("dir") or "/x") / "memory.jsonl"
    ks_lines = mine.read_lines(ks_mem_path)
    f["ks_memory_sha16_now"] = mine.sha16(ks_mem_path)
    f["ks_memory_sha16_at_freeze"] = ((ks.get("frozen") or {}).get("files") or {}).get("memory.jsonl")
    ids = [x["id"] for x in mine.save_lines(ks_lines)]
    f["next_id"] = max(ids) + 1 if ids else None
    s1_ids, s2_ids = mine.line_by_stamp(ks_lines, truths_a.get("S1")), mine.line_by_stamp(ks_lines, truths_a.get("S2"))
    f["s1_id"] = s1_ids[0] if len(s1_ids) == 1 else None
    f["s2_id"] = s2_ids[0] if len(s2_ids) == 1 else None
    f["ks_new"] = new_maps_a(a)
    # child B's frozen set
    fb = Path((r.get("frozen_b") or {}).get("dir") or "/x")
    b_path = fb / "memory.jsonl"
    b = r.get("b") or {}
    sw = b.get("save_watch") or {}
    f["b_memory_exists"] = b_path.is_file()
    if name == "CHAIN":
        f.update(rule_n_part(b, sw, known_a))
        f["b_reason"] = str((b.get("play") or {}).get("reason"))[:120]
    if not b_path.is_file():
        return f
    f["b_memory_prefix_is_ks_file"] = b_path.read_bytes().startswith(ks_mem_path.read_bytes())
    b_lines = mine.read_lines(b_path)
    b_session = next((x for x in reversed(b_lines) if x.get("kind") == "session"
                      and (x.get("run_dir") or "").rstrip("\\/").endswith("b_run")), None)
    f["b_continued_from"] = (b_session or {}).get("continued_from")
    f["b_restored_maps"] = sorted((b_session or {}).get("restored_maps") or [])
    mine_lines = sorted((x for x in mine.save_lines(b_lines) if f["next_id"] is not None and x["id"] >= f["next_id"]),
                        key=lambda x: x["id"])
    chain_items = []
    for x in mine_lines:
        payload, err, chain = mine.rebuild(b_lines, x["id"])
        last = ((payload or {}).get("goals") or {}).get("last_save") if payload else None
        chain_items.append({"id": x["id"], "parent": x.get("parent"), "stamp": x.get("play_time"), "error": err,
                            "chain": chain, "last_save": {k: (last or {}).get(k) for k in ("id", "play_time")} if last else None})
    f["b_lines"] = chain_items
    first = chain_items[0] if chain_items else None
    f["first_line"] = first
    bsaves = sw.get("saves") or []
    btruths = {s["save"]: s.get("truth") for s in bsaves}
    # determinism raw material: B's presses up to its FIRST save's e3
    e3_first = bsaves[0]["e3"] if bsaves else None
    f["presses_to_first_e3"] = [p[:2] for p in b.get("presses") or [] if e3_first is not None and p[0] is not None and p[0] < e3_first]
    f["first_save_e3"], f["first_save_e4"] = e3_first, (bsaves[0].get("e4") if bsaves else None)
    kill = b.get("kill") or {}
    f["kill_frame"], f["kill_spec"] = kill.get("frame"), kill.get("spec")
    f["killed"] = (r.get("child_b") or {}).get("word") == "READY" and kill.get("frame") is not None
    if name != "CHAIN":
        rel, key = ("game_memory.py", "memory_sha") if name == "CHAIN-OLD" else ("mode.py", "mode_sha")
        f["mutant"] = mutant_facts(r, b, rel, key)
        f["kill_is_first_e4_plus_40"] = f["first_save_e4"] is not None and kill.get("frame") == f["first_save_e4"] + OLD_KILL_AFTER_E4
        return f
    # rule N (computed above by rule_n_part)
    from_arrival = f["_from_arrival"]
    k = f["replay"].get("anchor")
    sk = next((s for s in bsaves if s["save"] == k), None) if k is not None else None
    sk_ids = mine.line_by_stamp(b_lines, (sk or {}).get("truth"), f["next_id"] or 0)
    f["sk_line_ids"] = sk_ids
    f["sk_id"] = sk_ids[0] if len(sk_ids) == 1 else None
    # maps
    first_b = mine.first_frames(from_arrival)
    e3k = (sk or {}).get("e3")
    b_pre = sorted(m for m, fr in first_b.items() if e3k is not None and fr <= e3k)
    known_k = set(known_a) | set(b_pre)
    f["NEW_B_pre"] = sorted(set(b_pre) - set(known_a))
    f["NEW_B_post"] = sorted(m for m, fr in first_b.items() if e3k is not None and kill.get("frame") is not None
                             and e3k < fr <= kill["frame"] and m not in known_k)
    f["ABANDONED"] = sorted(set(f["ks_new"]) - set(b_pre))
    # C1: MY parser on B's frozen cart
    p = mine.parse_sav(fb / "current_save.sav")
    f["C1"] = {"word": mine.file_word(p), "loaded": p.get("loaded"),
               "holds_sk": mine.loaded_is(p, (sk or {}).get("truth")),
               "holds_other_b_save": [s for s, t in btruths.items() if s != k and mine.loaded_is(p, t)],
               "holds_s1": mine.loaded_is(p, truths_a.get("S1"))}
    # C2
    f["C2"] = {"continued_from": f["b_continued_from"], "s1_id": f["s1_id"], "ident_s1": ident(b, truths_a.get("S1"))}
    # C3 (the FIRST B line, by id; the whole chain)
    f["C3"] = {"first_id_is_next_id": bool(first) and first["id"] == f["next_id"],
               "parent": (first or {}).get("parent"),
               "rebuilds": bool(first) and first["error"] is None,
               "last_save_own": bool(first) and first["last_save"] == {"id": first["id"], "play_time": first["stamp"]},
               "stamp_is_b_save1_truth": bool(first) and bool(btruths.get(1)) and list(first["stamp"] or [])[:2] == btruths[1]["play_time"][:2],
               "later_chain": all(chain_items[i]["parent"] == chain_items[i - 1]["id"] for i in range(1, len(chain_items))),
               "all_rebuild": all(x["error"] is None for x in chain_items),
               "all_last_save_own": all(x["last_save"] == {"id": x["id"], "play_time": x["stamp"]} for x in chain_items),
               "sk_line_found": f["sk_id"] is not None}
    # C4 (reported only)
    s1start = (bsaves[0].get("start") or {}) if bsaves else {}
    told = ""
    for src in (Path(r.get("b_run") or "/x") / "actions.jsonl", Path(r.get("b_run") or "/x").parent / "child_b.out"):
        if src.is_file():
            told += src.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    a_s1 = (truths_a.get("S1") or {}).get("play_time")
    b_s1 = (btruths.get(1) or {}).get("play_time")
    f["C4"] = {"held_free": s1start.get("held_free"), "overworld": s1start.get("overworld"), "at": s1start.get("const"),
               "gap_minutes": (b_s1[0] * 60 + b_s1[1]) - (a_s1[0] * 60 + a_s1[1]) if a_s1 and b_s1 else None,
               "restored_last_save": "exercised" if "[AI-SAVE] no save for" in told else "not exercised"}
    # C5 + C6 (child C)
    c = r.get("c") or {}
    c_lines = r.get("c_memory") or []
    c_session = mine.last_session(c_lines)
    mc = c.get("memory_check") or {}
    f["C5"] = {"memory_check_ok_killed": mc.get("ok") is True and mc.get("end_class") == "KILLED",
               "continued_from": (c_session or {}).get("continued_from"), "sk_id": f["sk_id"],
               "ident_sk": ident(c, (sk or {}).get("truth")), **menu_facts(c)} if c else {"missing": True}
    snap, why = restore_from_run(r.get("c_run"))
    snap_maps = sorted((snap or {}).get("maps", {}).keys()) if snap else None
    c_rest = sorted((c_session or {}).get("restored_maps") or [])
    f["C6"] = {"restore_reason": why, "snapshot_maps": snap_maps, "session_restored": c_rest,
               "agree": snap_maps is not None and snap_maps == c_rest,
               "holds_s1_set": set(f["b_restored_maps"]) <= set(c_rest),
               "holds_new_b_pre": set(f["NEW_B_pre"]) <= set(c_rest),
               "none_of_new_b_post": not (set(f["NEW_B_post"]) & set(c_rest)),
               "none_of_abandoned": not (set(f["ABANDONED"]) & set(c_rest))} if c else {"missing": True}
    return f


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R2-RETRY, R2-RETRY-OLD
def r2_facts(r: dict, kafter: dict | None) -> dict:
    name = r.get("name") or r["arm"]
    step1 = r.get("step1") or {}
    fz = Path((r.get("frozen_step1") or {}).get("dir") or "/x")
    f = {"arm": name}
    want = f"play: stuck: the continue stopped: the continued game did not reach a free overworld within {STOP_ARRIVAL} frames"
    launches = mine.read_lines(fz / "launches.jsonl") if (fz / "launches.jsonl").is_file() else []
    run_dir = str(step1.get("run_dir") or "")
    end = next((x for x in reversed(launches) if x.get("kind") == "launch_end" and x.get("run_dir") == run_dir), None)
    f["step1_reason"] = (end or {}).get("exit_reason")
    mem = mine.read_lines(fz / "memory.jsonl") if (fz / "memory.jsonl").is_file() else []
    sess_i = max((i for i, x in enumerate(mem) if x.get("kind") == "session"), default=None)
    sess = mem[sess_i] if sess_i is not None else None
    endl = next((x for x in mem if x.get("kind") == "end" and sess and x.get("session") == sess.get("n")), None)
    acts = Path(run_dir) / "actions.jsonl"
    cont = []
    if acts.is_file():
        for raw in acts.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            rec = json.loads(raw)
            if rec.get("decision") == "continue the saved game" and str(rec.get("action", "")).startswith("press A"):
                cont.append(rec.get("frame"))
    pur = Path(run_dir) / "purity.json"
    ka = kafter or {}
    ka_p = mine.parse_sav(Path((ka.get("frozen") or {}).get("dir") or "/x") / "current_save.sav") if ka else {"error": "x"}
    f["reach"] = {"kafter_file_s2": mine.file_word(ka_p) == "S2",
                  "launcher_line_is_the_arrival_stop": str(f["step1_reason"] or "").startswith(want),
                  "session_continued_nothing": bool(sess) and sess.get("how") == "continue" and sess.get("continued_from") is None
                  and sess.get("run_dir") == run_dir,
                  "end_stopped_before_restore": bool(endl) and endl.get("stopped_before_restore") is True and not endl.get("chosen"),
                  "played_nothing": sess_i is not None and not [x for x in mem[sess_i + 1:] if x.get("kind") in ("save", "lost")],
                  "continue_press_in_action_log": bool(cont)}
    f["purity"] = json.loads(pur.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("verdict") if pur.is_file() else None
    b = r.get("b") or {}
    mc = b.get("memory_check") or {}
    f["memory_check"] = {k: mc.get(k) for k in ("ok", "end_class", "looked_through")}
    f["memory_check_why"] = mc.get("why")
    if name == "R2-RETRY-OLD":
        f["mutant"] = mutant_facts(r, b, "host/launcher.py", "launcher_sha")
        after = Path((r.get("b_run") or "/x"))  # not used: the profile's memory after the check is read below
        prof = None
        for cand in Path(r["b_run"]).parents[2].glob(f"profiles/ai-r1live-r2-retry-old-b-{r['seed']}/ai_player/memory.jsonl"):
            prof = cand
        f["memory_sha_before_mine"] = mine.sha64(fz / "memory.jsonl")
        f["memory_sha_after_mine"] = mine.sha64(prof) if prof else None
        f["memory_byte_identical"] = f["memory_sha_before_mine"] is not None and f["memory_sha_before_mine"] == f["memory_sha_after_mine"]
        return f
    # S2's line id in K-AFTER's frozen memory, by identity (stamp = K-AFTER child A's S2 RAM truth)
    s2_truth = ((ka.get("a") or {}).get("truths") or {}).get("S2")
    ka_lines = mine.read_lines(Path((ka.get("frozen") or {}).get("dir") or "/x") / "memory.jsonl") if ka else []
    s2_ids = mine.line_by_stamp(ka_lines, s2_truth)
    f["s2_id"] = s2_ids[0] if len(s2_ids) == 1 else None
    bs = mine.last_session(r.get("b_memory"))
    f["b_continued_from"] = (bs or {}).get("continued_from")
    f["ident_s2"] = ident(b, s2_truth)
    f.update(menu_facts(b))
    words = str(mc.get("why") or "")
    f["R1"] = mc.get("ok") is True and mc.get("end_class") == "KILLED" and "looked through 1 " in words \
        and bool(f["step1_reason"]) and str(f["step1_reason"]) in words
    return f


ARMS_K = ("K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST", "K-BETWEEN", "K-BETWEEN-MID", "K-INSIDE-OLD", "K-SILENT-OLD")


def seed_facts(out: Path, seed: int) -> dict:
    S = {}
    for name in ("CONTROL",) + ARMS_K + ("LAUNCH", "LAUNCH-OLD", "CHAIN", "CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD", "R2-RETRY", "R2-RETRY-OLD"):
        r = row(out, name, seed)
        if r is None:
            S[name] = {"missing": True}
            continue
        if r.get("result"):
            S[name] = {"missing": True, "result": r["result"]}
            continue
        if name == "CONTROL":
            S[name] = control_facts(r)
        elif name.startswith("LAUNCH"):
            S[name] = launch_facts(r)
        elif name.startswith("CHAIN"):
            S[name] = chain_facts(r)
        elif name.startswith("R2"):
            S[name] = r2_facts(r, row(out, "K-AFTER", seed))
        else:
            S[name] = k_arm_facts(r)
    return S


def discriminators(S: dict) -> list[dict]:
    g = lambda n, k: (S.get(n) or {}).get(k)  # noqa: E731
    D = []
    for new, old in (("K-INSIDE", "K-INSIDE-OLD"), ("K-SILENT", "K-SILENT-OLD")):
        l, r_ = g(new, "NEW_in_restored"), g(old, "NEW_in_restored")
        D.append({"pair": f"{new}/{old}", "NEW_in_restored": [l, r_],
                  "continued_from": [g(new, "continued_from"), g(old, "continued_from")],
                  "differ": bool(g(new, "NEW")) and l != r_ and g(new, "continued_from") != g(old, "continued_from")})
    D.append({"pair": "K-AFTER/K-INSIDE", "FILE": [g("K-AFTER", "file"), g("K-INSIDE", "file")],
              "differ": g("K-AFTER", "file") is not None and g("K-AFTER", "file") != g("K-INSIDE", "file")})
    D.append({"pair": "LAUNCH/LAUNCH-OLD", "first_look": [g("LAUNCH", "first_look"), g("LAUNCH-OLD", "first_look")],
              "differ": g("LAUNCH", "first_look") is not None and g("LAUNCH", "first_look") != g("LAUNCH-OLD", "first_look")})
    D.append({"pair": "K-FIRST/CONTROL", "FILE": [g("K-FIRST", "file"), g("CONTROL", "file")],
              "differ": g("K-FIRST", "file") is not None and g("K-FIRST", "file") != g("CONTROL", "file")})
    D.append({"pair": "K-BETWEEN NEW vs restored", "NEW": g("K-BETWEEN", "NEW"), "NEW_in_restored": g("K-BETWEEN", "NEW_in_restored"),
              "differ": bool(g("K-BETWEEN", "NEW")) and not g("K-BETWEEN", "NEW_in_restored")})
    fl = lambda n: (S.get(n) or {}).get("first_line") or {}  # noqa: E731
    par = [fl("CHAIN").get("parent"), fl("CHAIN-OLD").get("parent")]
    D.append({"pair": "CHAIN/CHAIN-OLD", "parent": par, "differ": None not in par and par[0] != par[1]})
    ls = [(fl("CHAIN").get("last_save") or {}).get("id"), (fl("CHAIN-R1-OLD").get("last_save") or {}).get("id")]
    D.append({"pair": "CHAIN/CHAIN-R1-OLD", "last_save.id": ls, "differ": None not in ls and ls[0] != ls[1]})
    ok = [(g("R2-RETRY", "memory_check") or {}).get("ok"), (g("R2-RETRY-OLD", "memory_check") or {}).get("ok")]
    D.append({"pair": "R2-RETRY/R2-RETRY-OLD", "memory_check.ok": ok, "differ": None not in ok and ok[0] != ok[1]})
    return D


def main() -> int:
    out, first, last = Path(sys.argv[1]), int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3])
    report = {}
    for seed in range(first, last + 1):
        S = seed_facts(out, seed)
        D = discriminators(S)
        report[seed] = {"arms": S, "discriminators": D}
        print(f"===== seed {seed}")
        for n, f in S.items():
            brief = {k: v for k, v in f.items() if k not in ("presses_to_first_e3", "known_a", "b_lines", "_from_arrival")}
            print(f"  {n}: " + json.dumps(brief, default=str)[:2500])
        for d in D:
            print("  DISC " + json.dumps(d, default=str))
        base = (S.get("CHAIN") or {}).get("presses_to_first_e3")
        for old in ("CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD"):
            other = (S.get(old) or {}).get("presses_to_first_e3")
            print("  DETERMINISM " + json.dumps({"pair": f"CHAIN/{old}", "presses_to_B_first_save_e3": [len(base or []), len(other or [])],
                                                 "identical": base is not None and base == other,
                                                 "note": "one trajectory, replayed; not a replication"}))
    n = sum(1 for s in report.values() for d in s["discriminators"] if d["differ"])
    t = sum(len(s["discriminators"]) for s in report.values())
    (HERE / f"my-facts-{first}-{last}.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, default=str, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"MY DISCRIMINATORS ({first}-{last}): {n} of {t} differ")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

######## 15. MY INSTRUMENT: my_checks.py

==== MY INSTRUMENT: my_checks.py: my_checks.py (sha256 221f41781f3f9b468ec87f3c8ca8147d0218fe9ada10227457416a3bf1736d07, 11569 bytes) ====
"""My OWN per-arm grade and verdict from my_facts.py's facts (recomputed from the raw outputs), never from live_r1's
grade/summary/verdict. Every expectation comes from the arm's OWN record (orchestrator ruling 5).

    py -3.12 -I my_checks.py FACTS.json      (my-facts-FIRST-LAST.json)
Prints one line per check, a per-arm table (counted / pass or shows) and MY VERDICT: PASS needs every graded arm counted
on at least 3 of the 4 seeds, every counted NEW arm passing and every counted OLD arm showing (spec 5.4).
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

GRADED = ("CONTROL", "K-AFTER", "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-FIRST", "K-BETWEEN", "LAUNCH", "CHAIN", "R2-RETRY",
          "K-INSIDE-OLD", "K-SILENT-OLD", "LAUNCH-OLD", "CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD", "R2-RETRY-OLD")
OLD_OF = {"K-INSIDE-OLD": "K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT-OLD": "K-SILENT", "LAUNCH-OLD": "LAUNCH", "CHAIN-OLD": "CHAIN",
          "CHAIN-R1-OLD": "CHAIN", "R2-RETRY-OLD": "R2-RETRY"}
FLOOR = 3
CRASH = ("Traceback", "Error", "exception", "crash")


def grade_seed(A: dict) -> tuple[dict, list]:
    """Returns {arm: {counted, ok, checks{name: bool}, why}} and the check lines."""
    res, lines = {}, []

    def arm(name, counted, checks, why=""):
        ok = counted and all(checks.values())
        res[name] = {"counted": bool(counted), "ok": bool(ok), "checks": checks, "why": why}

    g = lambda n: A.get(n) or {}  # noqa: E731
    c = g("CONTROL")
    arm("CONTROL", not c.get("missing") and bool(c.get("end")),
        {"FILE = newest save": c.get("file") == f"S{c.get('saves')}", "loaded = newest truth": c.get("loaded_is_newest_truth") is True,
         "ended done at ch1_pokedex": "done: ch1_pokedex reached" in str(c.get("end"))})
    f = g("K-AFTER")
    n = (f.get("kill") or {}).get("new_sectors_at_kill")
    arm("K-AFTER", f.get("file") == "S2" and n == 14 and bool(f.get("NEW")),
        {"ALL of NEW restored": f.get("NEW_in_restored") == f.get("NEW"), "continued_from 2": f.get("continued_from") == 2,
         "arrival = S2": (f.get("arrival_eq") or {}).get("S2") is True, "no page": f.get("no_page") is True,
         "CONTINUE, no NEW GAME": f.get("continue_pressed") is True and f.get("no_new_game") is True})
    for name in ("K-INSIDE", "K-SILENT", "K-BETWEEN", "K-BETWEEN-MID"):
        f = g(name)
        k = f.get("kill") or {}
        n = k.get("new_sectors_at_kill")
        checks = {"NONE of NEW restored": not f.get("NEW_in_restored"), "continued_from 1": f.get("continued_from") == 1,
                  "arrival = S1": (f.get("arrival_eq") or {}).get("S1") is True,
                  "CONTINUE, no NEW GAME": f.get("continue_pressed") is True and f.get("no_new_game") is True}
        if name == "K-INSIDE":
            want = "S1+ERROR" if (n or 0) > 0 else "S1"
            counted = f.get("file") == want and bool(f.get("NEW")) and k.get("line_before_kill") is True
            if (n or 0) > 0:
                checks["CORRUPTED page"] = f.get("pages") == ["gText_SaveFileCorrupted"]
        elif name == "K-SILENT":
            counted = n == 0 and k.get("line_before_kill") is True and f.get("file") == "S1" and bool(f.get("NEW"))
            checks["no page"] = f.get("no_page") is True
        else:
            counted = bool(f.get("NEW")) and not f.get("missing")
            checks["FILE S1"] = f.get("file") == "S1"
            checks["no page"] = f.get("no_page") is True
        arm(name, counted, checks)
    f = g("K-FIRST")
    n = (f.get("kill") or {}).get("new_sectors_at_kill")
    arm("K-FIRST", f.get("file") == ("CORRUPT" if (n or 0) > 0 else "EMPTY"),
        {"ERASED page": f.get("pages") == ["gText_SaveFileErased"], "stops 'the save is gone'": "the save is gone" in str(f.get("b_reason")),
         "no NEW GAME": f.get("no_new_game") is True})
    for old, new in (("K-INSIDE-OLD", "K-INSIDE"), ("K-SILENT-OLD", "K-SILENT")):
        f, t = g(old), g(new)
        ran = "R1-M" in str(f.get("skills_saving")) and f.get("skills_saving_sha_mine") == f.get("skills_saving_sha") \
            and f.get("skills_saving_sha_mine") != t.get("skills_saving_sha_mine")
        arm(old, res[new]["counted"] and ran,
            {"restored holds ALL of NEW": bool(t.get("NEW")) and f.get("NEW_in_restored") == t.get("NEW"),
             "continued_from 2 (twin 1)": f.get("continued_from") == 2 and t.get("continued_from") == 1,
             "not a crash": not any(w in str(f.get("b_reason")) for w in CRASH)})
    L, LO = g("LAUNCH"), g("LAUNCH-OLD")
    fl = L.get("first_look") or [None, None]
    arm("LAUNCH", res["K-AFTER"]["counted"] and not L.get("missing"),
        {"first look a cold boot": fl[0] != "OVERWORLD" and fl[1] is not None and fl[1] < 300,
         "continued_from 2": L.get("continued_from") == 2, "purity CLEAN": L.get("purity") == "CLEAN"})
    flo = LO.get("first_look") or [None, None]
    arm("LAUNCH-OLD", res["LAUNCH"]["counted"] and LO.get("mutant") is True,
        {"first look OVERWORLD at frame >= 66883": flo[0] == "OVERWORLD" and (flo[1] or 0) >= 66883})
    # ---- CHAIN
    ch = g("CHAIN")
    ks_ok = res["K-SILENT"]["counted"]
    reach = {"K-SILENT counted": ks_ok, "rule N met (my replay)": bool((ch.get("replay") or {}).get("met")),
             "watcher's kill = my replay's": ch.get("watcher_agrees") is True,
             "S_k e4 before the kill": ch.get("sk_e4_before_kill") is True,
             "no START since S_k e4": ch.get("starts_since_sk_e4") == [], "NEW_B_pre non-empty": bool(ch.get("NEW_B_pre")),
             "known set = parent's": ch.get("known_a_equals_parents") is True}
    C1, C2, C3, C5, C6 = (ch.get(k) or {} for k in ("C1", "C2", "C3", "C5", "C6"))
    checks = {"C1 cart holds S_k by truth, no other B save, not +ERROR": C1.get("holds_sk") is True and C1.get("holds_other_b_save") == []
              and "+ERROR" not in str(C1.get("word")) and C1.get("holds_s1") is False,
              "C2 B continued S1 and arrived at S1's truth": C2.get("continued_from") == ch.get("s1_id") and ch.get("s1_id") is not None
              and (C2.get("ident_s1") or {}).get("ok") is True,
              "C3 first B line (by id) parent = S1 id, not S2 id": C3.get("first_id_is_next_id") is True and C3.get("parent") == ch.get("b_continued_from")
              and C3.get("parent") != ch.get("s2_id") and ch.get("s2_id") is not None,
              "C3 rebuilds, last_save is itself, stamp = RAM truth": C3.get("rebuilds") is True and C3.get("last_save_own") is True
              and C3.get("stamp_is_b_save1_truth") is True,
              "C3 whole post-continue chain": C3.get("later_chain") is True and C3.get("all_rebuild") is True
              and C3.get("all_last_save_own") is True and C3.get("sk_line_found") is True,
              "C3 B's frozen memory extends K-SILENT's file": ch.get("b_memory_prefix_is_ks_file") is True,
              "C5 C allowed (KILLED), continued S_k, arrived at S_k's truth, CONTINUE, no NEW GAME, no page":
                  C5.get("memory_check_ok_killed") is True and C5.get("continued_from") == C5.get("sk_id") and C5.get("sk_id") is not None
                  and (C5.get("ident_sk") or {}).get("ok") is True and C5.get("continue_pressed") is True and C5.get("no_new_game") is True
                  and C5.get("no_page") is True,
              "C6 snapshot = session restored; holds S1's set and NEW_B_pre": C6.get("agree") is True and C6.get("holds_s1_set") is True
              and C6.get("holds_new_b_pre") is True,
              "C6 (consistency) none of NEW_B_post nor ABANDONED": C6.get("none_of_new_b_post") is True and C6.get("none_of_abandoned") is True}
    arm("CHAIN", all(reach.values()), checks, why={k: v for k, v in reach.items() if not v})
    for old, field in (("CHAIN-OLD", "parent"), ("CHAIN-R1-OLD", "last_save")):
        f = g(old)
        first = f.get("first_line") or {}
        reach_o = {"K-SILENT counted": ks_ok, "killed at its first save's e4 + 40": f.get("kill_is_first_e4_plus_40") is True,
                   "mutant ran": (f.get("mutant") or {}).get("ok") is True, "twin CHAIN counted": res["CHAIN"]["counted"]}
        if field == "parent":
            shows = {"B's first line parent = S2's id": bool(first) and first.get("parent") == f.get("s2_id") and f.get("s2_id") is not None}
        else:
            shows = {"rebuilt last_save.id != own id": bool(first) and (first.get("last_save") or {}).get("id") != first.get("id")}
        arm(old, all(reach_o.values()), shows, why={k: v for k, v in reach_o.items() if not v})
        res[old]["shows_even_uncounted"] = all(shows.values())
    # ---- R2
    r2, r2o = g("R2-RETRY"), g("R2-RETRY-OLD")
    reach2 = r2.get("reach") or {}
    arm("R2-RETRY", bool(reach2) and all(reach2.values()),
        {"R1 allowed (KILLED), 'looked through 1' and step 1's reason": r2.get("R1") is True,
         "R2 continued S2, arrived at S2's truth, CONTINUE, no NEW GAME, no page": r2.get("b_continued_from") == r2.get("s2_id")
         and r2.get("s2_id") is not None and (r2.get("ident_s2") or {}).get("ok") is True and r2.get("continue_pressed") is True
         and r2.get("no_new_game") is True and r2.get("no_page") is True,
         "R3 step 1's purity CLEAN": r2.get("purity") == "CLEAN"}, why={k: v for k, v in reach2.items() if not v})
    reach2o = r2o.get("reach") or {}
    mc = r2o.get("memory_check") or {}
    arm("R2-RETRY-OLD", bool(reach2o) and all(reach2o.values()) and (r2o.get("mutant") or {}).get("ok") is True and res["R2-RETRY"]["counted"],
        {"refused, class CHOSEN": mc.get("ok") is False and mc.get("end_class") == "CHOSEN",
         "memory byte-identical": r2o.get("memory_byte_identical") is True})
    for name, r in res.items():
        for check, ok in r["checks"].items():
            lines.append((name, check, ok, r["counted"]))
    return res, lines


def main() -> int:
    rep = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    seeds = sorted(rep, key=int)
    per = {}
    bad = []
    for s in seeds:
        res, lines = grade_seed(rep[s]["arms"])
        per[s] = res
        for name, check, ok, counted in lines:
            tag = "ok " if ok else ("BAD" if counted else "n/c")
            print(f"{tag} {name:<14} {s} {check}")
            if counted and not ok:
                bad.append(f"{name}-{s} {check}")
        for name in ("CHAIN", "CHAIN-OLD", "CHAIN-R1-OLD", "R2-RETRY", "R2-RETRY-OLD"):
            if not res[name]["counted"]:
                print(f"NOT COUNTED {name}-{s}: {res[name]['why']}")
    print("MY ARMS")
    failing, below = [], []
    for name in GRADED + ("K-BETWEEN-MID",):
        cells, counted = [], 0
        for s in seeds:
            r = per[s][name]
            counted += r["counted"]
            cells.append(f"{s}:{'-' if not r['counted'] else ('ok' if r['ok'] else 'BAD')}")
            if r["counted"] and not r["ok"] and name in GRADED:
                failing.append(f"{name}-{s}")
        if name in GRADED and counted < FLOOR:
            below.append(name)
        print(f"  {name:<14} counted {counted}/{len(seeds)} " + " ".join(cells))
    verdict = "PASS" if not failing and not below else ("FAIL" if failing else "INCONCLUSIVE")
    disc = [d for s in seeds for d in rep[s]["discriminators"]]
    print(f"MY VERDICT ({seeds[0]}-{seeds[-1]}): {verdict} (failing: {failing or 'none'}; below the floor: {below or 'none'}; "
          f"my discriminators that differ: {sum(1 for d in disc if d['differ'])} of {len(disc)}; checks BAD on counted arms: {len(bad)})")
    return 0 if verdict == "PASS" else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

######## 16. MY INSTRUMENT'S FAULT TEST (on #58's seed 5551, BEFORE use)

==== MY INSTRUMENT'S FAULT TEST (on #58's seed 5551, BEFORE use): fault_mine.py (sha256 08a6420b95f53fb23d8c7a6ad7a4b6d26454a3d4c0f0313d2f5ac101caf70ad8, 13904 bytes) ====
"""FAULT + CONTROL test of MY instrument (mine.py, my_facts.py, my_checks.py), run BEFORE I rely on it, on stage 3's
recorded seed 5551 (#58's raw outputs; read only: every plant is an in-memory copy of a row, or a file copy in a temp
folder under this one). The CONTROL must grade clean; every planted fault must make the NAMED check (or the counting)
come out wrong. A planted fault that does not is a FAIL of the instrument.

    py -3.12 -I fault_mine.py STAGE3_HELDOUT_DIR
"""
import copy
import json
import shutil
import struct
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import mine  # noqa: E402
import my_checks  # noqa: E402
import my_facts  # noqa: E402

OUT = Path(sys.argv[1])
SEED = 5551
TMP = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="fault_mine_", dir=HERE))
results = []


def record(name, got_bad, detail=""):
    results.append((name, got_bad, detail))
    print(f"{'caught ' if got_bad else 'MISSED '} {name} {detail}")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- P: my flash parser
sav = Path(my_facts.row(OUT, "K-AFTER", SEED)["frozen"]["dir"]) / "current_save.sav"
raw = bytearray(sav.read_bytes())


def phys_of(buf, slot, sid):
    for phys in range(slot * 14, slot * 14 + 14):
        if struct.unpack_from("<H", buf, phys * mine.SECTOR + 0xFF4)[0] == sid:
            return phys
    raise SystemExit("no sector")


control_word = mine.file_word(mine.parse_sav_bytes(bytes(raw)))
print(f"CONTROL parser K-AFTER-{SEED}: {control_word}")
newest_slot = mine.parse_sav_bytes(bytes(raw))["loaded"]["slot"]
f1 = bytearray(raw)
f1[phys_of(f1, newest_slot, 0) * mine.SECTOR + 0x20] ^= 0xFF
w1 = mine.file_word(mine.parse_sav_bytes(bytes(f1)))
record("P1 one byte of save 2's sector 0 flipped -> not S2", w1 != "S2" and w1 == "S1+ERROR", w1)
f2 = bytearray(raw)
f2[0:28 * mine.SECTOR] = b"\xff" * (28 * mine.SECTOR)
w2 = mine.file_word(mine.parse_sav_bytes(bytes(f2)))
record("P2 both slots erased -> EMPTY", w2 == "EMPTY", w2)
f3 = bytearray(f1)
other = 1 - newest_slot
f3[other * 14 * mine.SECTOR:(other + 1) * 14 * mine.SECTOR] = b"\xff" * (14 * mine.SECTOR)
w3 = mine.file_word(mine.parse_sav_bytes(bytes(f3)))
record("P3 no complete slot -> CORRUPT", w3 == "CORRUPT", w3)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the clean facts and grade
clean = my_facts.seed_facts(OUT, SEED)
res, _ = my_checks.grade_seed(clean)
bad_clean = [(n, c) for n, r in res.items() for c, ok in r["checks"].items() if r["counted"] and not ok]
uncounted = [n for n in my_checks.GRADED if not res[n]["counted"]]
print(f"CONTROL grade seed {SEED}: counted all graded arms: {not uncounted} {uncounted}; BAD checks: {bad_clean}")
control_ok = control_word == "S2" and not bad_clean and not uncounted
CH = my_facts.row(OUT, "CHAIN", SEED)
R2 = my_facts.row(OUT, "R2-RETRY", SEED)
R2O = my_facts.row(OUT, "R2-RETRY-OLD", SEED)
KA = my_facts.row(OUT, "K-AFTER", SEED)


def regrade(name, facts):
    arms = copy.deepcopy(clean)
    arms[name] = facts
    return my_checks.grade_seed(arms)[0]


def chain_with(plant=None, memory_from=None, memory_edit=None):
    r = copy.deepcopy(CH)
    if memory_from or memory_edit:
        d = TMP / f"fb{len(results)}"
        d.mkdir()
        src = Path(r["frozen_b"]["dir"])
        shutil.copy2(src / "current_save.sav", d / "current_save.sav")
        mem = Path(memory_from) if memory_from else src / "memory.jsonl"
        text = mem.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if memory_edit:
            text = memory_edit(text)
        (d / "memory.jsonl").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        r["frozen_b"]["dir"] = str(d)
    if plant:
        plant(r)
    return my_facts.chain_facts(r)


def expect_check(label, name, facts, check_prefix):
    g = regrade(name, facts)[name]
    hit = [c for c, ok in g["checks"].items() if c.startswith(check_prefix) and not ok]
    record(label, g["counted"] and bool(hit), f"(counted {g['counted']}, failing {hit})")


def expect_uncounted(label, name, facts):
    g = regrade(name, facts)[name]
    record(label, not g["counted"], f"(why {g['why']})")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- R: my reader on real and planted memory lines
old_mem = Path(my_facts.row(OUT, "CHAIN-OLD", SEED)["frozen_b"]["dir"]) / "memory.jsonl"
r1old_mem = Path(my_facts.row(OUT, "CHAIN-R1-OLD", SEED)["frozen_b"]["dir"]) / "memory.jsonl"
expect_check("R1 CH1-built live lines (CHAIN-OLD's file) read as CHAIN's: C3 parent", "CHAIN",
             chain_with(memory_from=old_mem), "C3 first B line")
expect_check("R2 FX1-built live lines (CHAIN-R1-OLD's file) read as CHAIN's: C3 last_save", "CHAIN",
             chain_with(memory_from=r1old_mem), "C3 rebuilds")


def reparent(line_id, new_parent):
    def edit(text):
        out = []
        for raw_line in text.splitlines():
            x = json.loads(raw_line) if raw_line.strip() else None
            if x and x.get("kind") == "save" and x.get("id") == line_id:
                x["parent"] = new_parent
                raw_line = json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False)
            out.append(raw_line)
        return "\n".join(out) + "\n"
    return edit


expect_check("R3 B's later line 4 re-parented to S1: C3 whole chain", "CHAIN",
             chain_with(memory_edit=reparent(4, 1)), "C3 whole")


def bad_del(text):
    out = []
    for raw_line in text.splitlines():
        x = json.loads(raw_line) if raw_line.strip() else None
        if x and x.get("kind") == "save" and x.get("id") == 3 and not x.get("full"):
            x["memory"].setdefault("del", []).append(["goals", "no_such_key_planted"])
            raw_line = json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False)
        out.append(raw_line)
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


expect_check("R4 a delete of a missing key in line 3 (strict reader): C3 rebuilds", "CHAIN",
             chain_with(memory_edit=bad_del), "C3 rebuilds")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- N: rule N
sk_save = clean["CHAIN"]["sk"]["save"]
sk = next(s for s in CH["b"]["save_watch"]["saves"] if s["save"] == sk_save)
entered = CH["b"]["kill"]["entered"]


def add_start(r):
    r["b"]["save_watch"]["starts"].append({"f": (sk["e4"] + entered) // 2, "held_free": True})


expect_uncounted("N1 a START between S_k's e4 and the new map: not counted", "CHAIN", chain_with(add_start))


def watcher_anchor_1(r):
    r["b"]["kill"]["anchor"] = 1


expect_uncounted("N2 the watcher's kill names anchor 1: not counted", "CHAIN", chain_with(watcher_anchor_1))


def no_new_pre(r):
    # every map B stood on before S_k's e3 becomes one child A knew: NEW_B_pre empty
    a = r["ks"]["a"]
    for f, key in r["b"]["save_watch"]["maps_from_arrival"]:
        a["maps"].insert(0, [0, key])


expect_uncounted("N3 NEW_B_pre empty: not counted (vacuous)", "CHAIN", chain_with(no_new_pre))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C: C1, C2, C5, C6
def sk_tile_off(r):
    t = next(s for s in r["b"]["save_watch"]["saves"] if s["save"] == sk_save)["truth"]
    t["pos"] = [t["pos"][0] + 1, t["pos"][1]]


expect_check("C1 S_k's truth one tile off: C1", "CHAIN", chain_with(sk_tile_off), "C1")


def b_arrival_off(r):
    r["b"]["arrival"]["map"] = [0, 9]


expect_check("C2 B's arrival on another map: C2", "CHAIN", chain_with(b_arrival_off), "C2")


def c_continued_other(r):
    for x in reversed(r["c_memory"]):
        if x.get("kind") == "session":
            x["continued_from"] = 3
            break


expect_check("C5 C's session continued B's FIRST line, not S_k's: C5", "CHAIN", chain_with(c_continued_other), "C5")


def c_check_refused(r):
    r["c"]["memory_check"]["ok"] = False


expect_check("C5b C's memory_check refused: C5", "CHAIN", chain_with(c_check_refused), "C5")


def c_drop_new_pre(r):
    drop = clean["CHAIN"]["NEW_B_pre"][0]
    for x in reversed(r["c_memory"]):
        if x.get("kind") == "session":
            x["restored_maps"] = [m for m in x["restored_maps"] if m != drop]
            break


expect_check("C6 C's restored set lacks a NEW_B_pre map: C6", "CHAIN", chain_with(c_drop_new_pre), "C6 snapshot")


def c_add_post(r):
    add = clean["CHAIN"]["NEW_B_post"][0]
    for x in reversed(r["c_memory"]):
        if x.get("kind") == "session":
            x["restored_maps"] = sorted(set(x["restored_maps"]) | {add})
            break


expect_check("C6b C's restored set holds a NEW_B_post map: C6 consistency", "CHAIN", chain_with(c_add_post), "C6 (consistency)")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- OLD arms
f = copy.deepcopy(clean["CHAIN-OLD"])
f["first_line"]["parent"] = f["s1_id"]
expect_check("O1 CHAIN-OLD's line parent = S1 (the mutant did nothing): shows", "CHAIN-OLD", f, "B's first line")
f = copy.deepcopy(clean["CHAIN-R1-OLD"])
f["first_line"]["last_save"]["id"] = f["first_line"]["id"]
expect_check("O2 CHAIN-R1-OLD's last_save is its own: shows", "CHAIN-R1-OLD", f, "rebuilt")
r = copy.deepcopy(my_facts.row(OUT, "CHAIN-OLD", SEED))
r["mutant"]["edits"][0][2] = "a replacement text that is not in the file"
expect_uncounted("O3 CHAIN-OLD's mutant edit not in the file it ran: not counted", "CHAIN-OLD", my_facts.chain_facts(r))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- R2-RETRY, R2-RETRY-OLD
r = copy.deepcopy(R2)
r["step1"]["run_dir"] = r["step1"]["run_dir"] + "_elsewhere"
expect_uncounted("Q1 step 1's launcher line is not this run's: not counted", "R2-RETRY", my_facts.r2_facts(r, KA))
r = copy.deepcopy(R2)
r["b"]["memory_check"]["why"] = r["b"]["memory_check"]["why"].replace("looked through 1 ", "looked through 0 ")
expect_check("Q2 step 2's words lack 'looked through 1': R1", "R2-RETRY", my_facts.r2_facts(r, KA), "R1")
r = copy.deepcopy(R2)
r["b"]["arrival"]["pos"] = [0, 0]
expect_check("Q3 step 2's arrival off S2's tile: R2", "R2-RETRY", my_facts.r2_facts(r, KA), "R2")
d = TMP / "step1_run"
shutil.copytree(R2["step1"]["run_dir"], d)
(d / "purity.json").write_text(json.dumps({"verdict": "NOT CLEAN"}), encoding="utf-8")
fz = TMP / "frozen_step1"
shutil.copytree(R2["frozen_step1"]["dir"], fz)
lines = (fz / "launches.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace(json.dumps(R2["step1"]["run_dir"])[1:-1], json.dumps(str(d))[1:-1])
(fz / "launches.jsonl").write_text(lines, encoding="utf-8")
mem = (fz / "memory.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace(json.dumps(R2["step1"]["run_dir"])[1:-1], json.dumps(str(d))[1:-1])
(fz / "memory.jsonl").write_text(mem, encoding="utf-8")
r = copy.deepcopy(R2)
r["step1"]["run_dir"], r["frozen_step1"]["dir"] = str(d), str(fz)
expect_check("Q4 step 1's purity.json NOT CLEAN (a copy of the run): R3", "R2-RETRY", my_facts.r2_facts(r, KA), "R3")
r = copy.deepcopy(R2O)
r["b"]["memory_check"]["ok"] = True
expect_check("Q5 R2-RETRY-OLD allowed: shows", "R2-RETRY-OLD", my_facts.r2_facts(r, KA), "refused")
f = copy.deepcopy(clean["R2-RETRY-OLD"])
f["memory_byte_identical"] = False
expect_check("Q6 R2-RETRY-OLD's memory changed: shows", "R2-RETRY-OLD", f, "memory byte")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the existing arms (facts-level plants)
def planted(name, key, value):
    f = copy.deepcopy(clean[name])
    node = f
    for k in key[:-1]:
        node = node[k]
    node[key[-1]] = value
    return f


expect_check("E1 K-INSIDE restores a NEW map (the R1-M bug on the NEW arm)", "K-INSIDE",
             planted("K-INSIDE", ["NEW_in_restored"], clean["K-INSIDE"]["NEW"][:1]), "NONE of NEW")
expect_uncounted("E2 K-AFTER's FILE S1: not counted", "K-AFTER", planted("K-AFTER", ["file"], "S1"))
expect_uncounted("E3 K-SILENT has a sector complete at the kill: not counted", "K-SILENT",
                 planted("K-SILENT", ["kill", "new_sectors_at_kill"], 1))
expect_uncounted("E4 K-BETWEEN's NEW empty: not counted (vacuous)", "K-BETWEEN", planted("K-BETWEEN", ["NEW"], []))
expect_check("E5 K-INSIDE-OLD restored none of NEW: shows", "K-INSIDE-OLD", planted("K-INSIDE-OLD", ["NEW_in_restored"], []),
             "restored holds")
expect_check("E6 LAUNCH-OLD's first look a cold boot: shows", "LAUNCH-OLD", planted("LAUNCH-OLD", ["first_look"], ["UNKNOWN", 0]),
             "first look")
expect_check("E7 CONTROL's FILE older than its newest save", "CONTROL", planted("CONTROL", ["file"], "S1"), "FILE")
expect_check("E8 LAUNCH's purity NOT CLEAN", "LAUNCH", planted("LAUNCH", ["purity"], "NOT CLEAN"), "purity")
expect_check("E9 K-FIRST read no ERASED page", "K-FIRST", planted("K-FIRST", ["pages"], []), "ERASED")
expect_uncounted("E10 K-SILENT-OLD's skills/saving.py hash not the mutant's: not counted", "K-SILENT-OLD",
                 planted("K-SILENT-OLD", ["skills_saving_sha_mine"], "0000000000000000"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- discriminators
D = my_facts.discriminators(clean)
arms = copy.deepcopy(clean)
arms["CHAIN-OLD"]["first_line"]["parent"] = arms["CHAIN"]["first_line"]["parent"]
d1 = [x for x in my_facts.discriminators(arms) if x["pair"] == "CHAIN/CHAIN-OLD"][0]
record("D1 CHAIN-OLD parent planted equal to CHAIN's: the pair no longer differs", not d1["differ"], json.dumps(d1))
arms = copy.deepcopy(clean)
arms["R2-RETRY-OLD"]["memory_check"]["ok"] = True
d2 = [x for x in my_facts.discriminators(arms) if x["pair"] == "R2-RETRY/R2-RETRY-OLD"][0]
record("D2 R2-RETRY-OLD ok planted True: the pair no longer differs", not d2["differ"], json.dumps(d2))

shutil.rmtree(TMP)
missed = [n for n, ok, _ in results if not ok]
print(f"CONTROL clean: {control_ok}; clean discriminators that differ: {sum(1 for x in D if x['differ'])} of {len(D)}")
ok = control_ok and not missed
print(f"MY INSTRUMENT FAULT TEST: {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} (control clean {control_ok}; {len(results) - len(missed)} of "
      f"{len(results)} planted faults caught; missed: {missed or 'none'})")
sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
